=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def findSubstring(self, s: str, words: List[str]) -> List[int]:
        wlen= len(words[0])
        slen= wlen*len(words)
        track=dict()
        
        occ = collections.Counter(words)

        def test():
            for key, val in track.items():
                if val !=occ[key]:
                    return False
            return True
        res=[]
        #first look

        for k in range(wlen):
            for i in words:
                track.update({i : 0})
            for i in range(k,slen+k,wlen):
                w=s[i:i+wlen]
                if w in words:
                    track.update({w: track[w]+1})
            if test():
                res.append(k)
            #complete
            for i in range(wlen+k, len(s)-slen+1,wlen):
                
                nw=s[i+slen-wlen:i+slen]
                pw=s[i-wlen:i]
                if nw in words:
                    track.update({nw: track[nw]+1})
                if pw in words:
                    track.update({pw: track[pw]-1})
                if test():
                    res.append(i+wlen)
        return res

```python
class Solution:
    def findSubstring(self, s: str, words: List[str]) -> List[int]:
        wlen= len(words[0])
        slen= wlen*len(words)
        track=dict()
        
        occ = collections.Counter(words)

        def test():
            for key, val in track.items():
                if val !=occ[key]:
                    return False
            return True
        res=[]
        #first look

        for k in range(wlen):
            for i in words:
                track.update({i : 0})
            for i in range(k,slen+k,wlen):
                w=s[i:i+wlen]
                if w in words:
                    track.update({w: track[w]+1})
            if test():
                res.append(k)
            #complete
            for i in range(wlen+k, len(s)-slen+1,wlen):
                
                nw=s[i+slen-wlen:i+slen]
                pw=s[i-wlen:i]
                if nw in words:
                    track.update({nw: track[nw]+1})
                if pw in words:
                    track.update({pw: track[pw]-1})
                if test():
                    res.append(i+wlen)
        return res
```

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

Let's analyze original code.

```python
class Solution:
    def findSubstring(self, s: str, words: List[str]) -> List[int]:
        wlen= len(words[0])
        slen= wlen*len(words)
        track=dict()
        
        occ = collections.Counter(words)

        def test():
            for key, val in track.items():
                if val !=occ[key]:
                    return False
            return True
        res=[]
        #first look

        for k in range(wlen):
            for i in words:
                track.update({i : 0})
            for i in range(k,slen+k,wlen):
                w=s[i:i+wlen]
                if w in words:
                    track.update({w: track[w]+1})
            if test():
                res.append(k)
            #complete
            for i in range(wlen+k, len(s)-slen+1,wlen):
                
                nw=s[i+slen-wlen:i+slen]
                pw=s[i-wlen:i]
                if nw in words:
                    track.update({nw: track[nw]+1})
                if pw in words:
                    track.update({pw: track[pw]-1})
                if test():
                    res.append(i+wlen)
        return res
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
import collections
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findSubstring(self, s: str, words: List[str]) -> List[int]:
        if not s or not words:
            return []

        wlen = len(words[0])
        slen = wlen * len(words)
        occ = collections.Counter(words)
        res = []

        def matches(track):
            return track == occ

        for k in range(wlen):
            track = {word: 0 for word in words}

            for i in range(k, slen + k, wlen):
                w = s[i:i + wlen]
                if w in occ:
                    track[w] += 1

            if matches(track):
                res.append(k)

            for i in range(wlen + k, len(s) - slen + 1, wlen):
                nw = s[i + slen - wlen:i + slen]
                pw = s[i - wlen:i]

                if nw in occ:
                    track[nw] += 1
                if pw in occ:
                    track[pw] -= 1

                if matches(track):
                    res.append(i)

        return sorted(res)
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
import collections
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findSubstring(self, s: str, words: List[str]) -> List[int]:
        if not s or not words:
            return []

        wlen = len(words[0])
        slen = wlen * len(words)
        occ = collections.Counter(words)
        res = []

        def matches(track):
            return track == occ

        for k in range(wlen):
            track = {word: 0 for word in words}

            for i in range(k, slen + k, wlen):
                w = s[i:i + wlen]
                if w in occ:
                    track[w] += 1

            if matches(track):
                res.append(k)

            for i in range(wlen + k, len(s) - slen + 1, wlen):
                nw = s[i + slen - wlen:i + slen]
                pw = s[i - wlen:i]

                if nw in occ:
                    track[nw] += 1
                if pw in occ:
                    track[pw] -= 1

                if matches(track):
                    res.append(i)

        return sorted(res)
